# Fit audit: silent failures across all fitters (TASK-101.7.1)

The audit completed **11185 scheduled cases, 99 families and 579 supported held subsets**. It found **533 strict-target silent-failure cases** (493 distinct input/hold witnesses) across **51 families**. loud failure: 168, non-Ok: 2203, ok: 8244, open: 37, silent failure: 533. Case counts are stress-grid hits, not prevalence estimates or counts of independent defects.

## Findings that warrant fixes

The clearest objective failures include:

- **Held-scale beta-prime:** direct log-beta/likelihood subtraction gives large errors on nearly constant data. Case 248 reports logLik 80 instead of 84.441950547…; case 249 reports about -5.05e10 instead of 130.493688857…. The relevant calculation is `betapr/mle.hpp:130–131`, calling `special/special.hpp:83–87`.
- **Held-shape NSBeta:** nearby digamma subtraction falsely brackets a shape root; lgamma subtraction also corrupts logLik. The held path in `nsbeta/mle.hpp:23–25` bypasses the repaired beta path and does not forward iterlim to its inner helper.
- **Transformed gamma families:** `invgamma/mle.hpp:49–53`, `lgamma/mle.hpp:23–28`, and `naka/mle.hpp:45–55` round reciprocal/log/squared observations before delegating. Nearly constant raw observations lose information before the stable gamma solver receives them.
- **Held Burr shape:** `burr/mle.hpp:310,344,351` amplifies rounded log differences at large power. The returned-point likelihood and high-precision stationary point disagree on tightly clustered data.

For the NSBeta witness (case 5348), shape2 is returned as **4.27914928881e+14**, while the unique held-shape reference is **4.73493829873e+150**. The reference MLE logLik is **4145.549562030086**, versus reported **-3367.3720572377251**. Its bracket was computed at 350 digits and its signs retained at 700 digits. Strict concavity in the free shape establishes uniqueness; the sign evaluations remain numerical. See [the focused reference](2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/focused-reference.json).

The coefficient-only category is deliberately separate: it violates the brief’s 1e-8 coefficient target even when likelihood loss is tiny and the fitter’s documented default tolerance is looser. Those rows are not evidence of a beta-prime-sized objective error. Their local information and stationarity checks are recorded. The final residual/information check also requires a linearized relative root-error proxy below 1e-10 and below one hundredth of the measured coefficient displacement; this is a numerical local consistency check, not a rigorous curvature enclosure (`root-precision-check.json`).


The seven largest data files (`cases.json`, `cases-simbox.json`, `reference.jsonl`, `static.json`, `fits.csv`, `fits-simbox.csv`, `fits-initial.csv`) are stored gzipped in the data directory; decompress them before replaying the scripts, which use the plain names.

## Method

The standalone C++17 harness reuses `dev/validation/diagnostics.py` and the shared binding call routes. It observes the core result before binding error conversion, retains 17 digits of each returned double, and preserves default controls and golden held values. All declared structural `holds:` exclusions are omitted exactly as in that sweep. The checkout has 99 golden-backed fitters, including `st`, rather than the 98 in the brief. No fitter, golden, dossier, binding or production test was changed.

The audit-local generator adapts diagnostic error callbacks to the current structured-error interface and repairs the GB2 observer coefficient mapping. These changes are confined to the generated audit harness; the shared fitter routes, their default controls and production sources are unchanged.

Samples include 12-observation interior subsets; n = 2, 3, 5; heavy ties; nearly constant samples at distinct valid locations with relative spread 1e-4, 1e-8, 1e-12; endpoint distances 1e-6, 1e-12, 1e-17, 1e-200 where representable; exact interior ULP complements; scales 1e-150 and 1e150; location offsets 1e8; concentration/outlier probes; and large, nearly constant and zero-but-one counts. Conditional support expressions are evaluated from metadata. The initial generic fallback for parameters named min/max was found insufficient; `support_samples.py` adds the missing endpoint and valid-location cases. Incompatible held-support samples remain as explicit rejection controls.

There are also 118 finite parameter sequences toward named faces in 44 families (K = 100 and 1e6 where representable). SciPy quantiles generate data only. Coupled-quantile convolution sequences are labelled as data sequences, not exact convolution quantiles. The exact binary64 `cases.json.gz` is frozen for replay. Held values are the golden values; this does not exhaust the continuum of possible held values.

The reference parser is `quality/density.py`, explicitly using README `log_density` rather than golden-specific `spec.py`. Inputs and returned doubles are converted exactly to mpmath. Working precision is at least 70 digits and escalates with magnitude; stable log1p rewrites, interval masses, centered CMP sums with geometric tail bounds, Hurwitz-zeta identities, atomic members and the implicit Tukey density are in `reference_extra.py`. Strict support exclusions are proved separately with exact rational representations of binary64 inputs and coefficients (`support_geometry.py`); equality at endpoints is not classified by that check. Such witnesses have exact zero likelihood at the returned point, even when the gap is only one ULP. They call for feasibility projection, not a claim that a conditional exact-arithmetic certificate has been disproved. Silent finite likelihood/improvement witnesses are checked again at doubled precision. This is numerical evidence, not an outward-rounded error enclosure.

The logLik threshold is 1e-8 × max(1, |reported logLik|). The search evaluates scaled free-coordinate scores, feasible coordinate moves, adjacent integer moves, a feasible golden seed, location/data-extreme probes, and up to twelve high-precision Newton iterations. Score findings require magnitude above 1e-3 and a feasible ascent witness. A coefficient-only finding requires error above 1e-8, a better point, per-observation stationarity residual below 1e-18, and positive local information with minimum eigenvalue above 1e-35 × max(1, maximum eigenvalue). Positive parameters use ordinary relative error; signed coordinates use max(1, |reference|) in the denominator. For narrow truncated-normal windows, the mean-coordinate probe uses sd²/window width, the natural tilt scale; ordinary data-scale probes can miss a large score. Nonsmooth/bound/integer coordinates are not treated as smooth root coordinates. Tukey-lambda uses 260 inversion bisections; its implicit derivatives are not used.

The initial core wall guard is three seconds, followed by twenty-second retries. Initial reference guards are two seconds, with targeted retries at 10–20 seconds. These are audit guards, not changes to iterlim. A wall-guarded call has no observed native terminal status and remains open. Finite-MLE claims for stopped fits use the sufficient conditions in `existence.py`, including dossier results, fixed-shape coercivity/tie conditions, or finite likelihood at the sole fully held point. The truncated-normal criterion in `tnorm_existence.py` uses strict concavity of the quadratic exponential family and compares its empirical sufficient statistic with the exponential/uniform boundary; high-precision signs carry a stated margin. Nonexistence/incompatible support proofs are recorded separately. A finite start or a local maximum alone is never used to prove global attainment.

The audit targets core statuses. For example, the Weibull, inverse-Weibull and Gumbel binding routes reject nonfinite logLik, so some nonfinite core-Ok findings are caught before an R/JS result is returned. The GED route checks coefficients but not logLik at that check. Binding-wide propagation beyond these inspected routes is not a claim of this audit.

## Per-family summary

`E` is the maximum of relative reported-logLik error, demonstrated relative likelihood gain, and verified isolated-reference coefficient error. These are different diagnostics. Infinity marks an invalid/nonfinite Ok payload or an exact returned-point support exclusion, not a finite measured error. A dash means no error value was evaluated. Status 0 is native Ok; -1 is implicit Ok for a result without a status member; -997 is the audit wall guard; -998/-999 mean missing process/core observation. Other codes are native family enums. “ok” includes successful numerical checks and proved nonexistence/incompatibility stops; it is not a global certificate for arbitrary data.

## Static scan

The recursive scan covers fitter headers and included helpers (excluding dpqr.hpp), with 764 nominations: {'log complement': 15, 'step stop': 334, 'fixed iteration': 109, 'special difference': 203, 'special start': 72, 'legacy': 29, 'one-pass variance': 2}. [The full file:line inventory](2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/static-inventory.md) gives each matched line, contextual review and dynamic family-level evidence.

Important distinctions: the one-pass log variance at `kumar/mle.hpp:151` is cancellation-prone, but fallback/certificate paths can protect it; its mere presence is not a demonstrated public-fit defect. `core/newton.hpp:12,36–40,59` records legacy parity and step-only termination, requiring caller-specific score/bracket review. The fixed hundred-step bisection at `ztpois/mle.hpp:17–22` has no convergence/exhaustion gate; no harmful case was established here. Small fixed matrix loops are not numerical iteration caps. `nbinom/mle.hpp:168` and `zip/mle.hpp:136` contain direct log complements, but execution paths and later gates matter.

The demonstrated held-scale beta-prime path reaches the direct lgamma difference in `special::lbeta`. A future stable shared log-beta change would also require checking its density callers. This audit did not change or audit settled dpqr/truth/codegen outputs.

## Ranked fix list

All silent categories precede loud failures. Within that ordering, finite wrong likelihoods / meaningful ascent on plausible samples rank above strict coefficient-accuracy misses and nonfinite payloads on extreme scales; breadth of held subsets and sample classes breaks ties. Frequency in this constructed grid is not real-data prevalence. Cause descriptions outside the specifically traced paths remain hypotheses.

| Rank | Family | Silent / loud cases | Held subsets affected | Exposure | Worst E | Fix direction / source |
|---|---|---|---:|---|---:|---|
| 1 | fpareto | 49 / 0 | 12 | plausible small/tied/clustered/offset data; constant, face, interior, limit, lower, scale, ties, tiny | 4.34e-06 | `univariateML-cpp/include/cppunivariate/distributions/fpareto/mle.hpp:9`: inspect the executed estimator/likelihood path; a more specific causal line is not established by this witness. |
| 2 | gb2 | 27 / 0 | 11 | plausible small/tied/clustered/offset data; constant, face, interior, lower, scale, ties, tiny | 1.87e-05 | Candidate seam `univariateML-cpp/include/cppunivariate/distributions/gb2/mle.hpp:83` (special difference): `double c=hb ? 0 : special::digamma(q)-special::digamma(p);`. Execution-path causation remains open. |
| 3 | tnorm | 2 / 29 | 9 | plausible small/tied/clustered/offset data; constant, limit, offset, scale | 1.22e-07 | `univariateML-cpp/include/cppunivariate/distributions/tnorm/mle.hpp:411`: The held mean/sd likelihood standardizes a and b separately before computing the mass; on a 1e-12-wide window this rounds away part of its width. The raw-endpoint high-precision interval mass gives a different returned-point logLik. This is the suspected arithmetic seam. |
| 4 | gev | 20 / 0 | 7 | plausible small/tied/clustered/offset data; constant, interior, limit, lower, offset, scale, tiny | inf | `univariateML-cpp/include/cppunivariate/distributions/gev/mle.hpp:263`: Affine restoration rounds mu and sigma independently. On the xi=-1 face the exact restored upper endpoint mu+sigma can fall below the largest observation. Recheck feasibility against the original data after restoration. Other witnesses require their own acceptance-path review. |

For the traced NSBeta path, delegate held shapes to the stable beta machinery and forward iterlim. For beta-prime and transformed-gamma paths, preserve raw-data likelihood information through transformations and evaluate the likelihood stably at the returned point. For coefficient-only rows, a tighter accuracy gate or a documented accuracy-policy decision is appropriate; changing defaults/goldens was outside this job. Some loud TransformFailure rows reflect an unrepresentable real-valued scale and require no promise of a representable estimate.

## Open entries and coverage limits

The [open register](2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/open.md) enumerates all 37 numerical gaps and all 2203 stops whose finite attainment/escape was not established. It quotes the relevant dossier existence statements and lists every declared limit, candidate sequence and missing face-specific work. A reported non-Ok candidate is not evidence of finite attainment.

This is a completed scheduled stress census, not a proof of absence of further failures. Local curvature/refinement is not a global certificate; the feasible-seed comparisons do not exhaust multimodal profiles or unbounded integer searches. The named-face sequences do not cover every held-value combination or prove that every generating sample has a finite interior MLE. Fully held infinite normal bounds, for example, do not exhaust finite-window held-normal regimes. Integer-location families use large-count stresses rather than a separate 1e8 translation. Inference, density/quantile/sampling accuracy, malformed inputs and all possible hold values are outside this audit.

## Reproduce and validation

From the worktree root, `bash dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/run.sh` rebuilds the routes and replays the frozen cases, retries guarded core calls, runs the reference and finding checks, validates the census and renders this report. It acquires CPU slots internally; do not wrap that shell script in another slot lock. Results resume by case ID. Remove fit/reference caches before applying the audit to changed production code or a newly generated case grid. `AUDIT_RESET_CASES=1` is for constructing a new grid, not replaying the recorded one.

Individual commands (each numerical command goes through `/tmp/umvl-cpu/run`):

- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/reproduce.py INDEX`: one exact core call and saved independent evidence.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/check_reference.py`: stable-oracle integral, normalization, Jacobian and extreme-parameter checks.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/check_findings.py`: local information/stationarity validation and mathematical stop classification.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/focused_references.py`: unique held-shape NSBeta reference.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/audit.py static`: recursive source inventory.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/validate.py`: completeness, status consistency, independent simple likelihood checks and source fingerprints.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/report.py`: render the report from validated results.
- `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/finalize.py`: check the frozen inventory and script syntax, validate the census, render the report and check its artifact links.
- Grid construction only: `AUDIT_RESET_CASES=1 python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/audit.py generate`, then `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/limit_samples.py`, `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/additional_samples.py`, and `python3 dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/support_samples.py`. Each mutates the inventory; clear derived caches first. Replay the frozen inventory for the exact recorded census. The historical inventory includes retained rejection controls from the earlier generator, so a fresh constructed grid is a new census.

Validation is **PASS**: 41 independent oracle checks; 142 independently written normal/exponential/Poisson likelihood checks; complete family/subset/case accounting; and a status-consistent reference for every observed core result. All coefficient-only silent witnesses passed the local information/stationarity gate. Full working precision and source SHA-256 values are retained in the data directory.

Machine record: the primary native C++ sweep and added support cases used the workstation (GCC 15); simbox (GCC 13) supplied a second native sweep. High-precision passes ran on the workstation and simbox, always against the saved workstation inputs/results for the primary census. Both machines passed oracle checks. `run-provenance.json` records the runs and budgets. Every compute worker used one BLAS/OpenMP thread, nice priority and the supplied reservation wrapper. No packages were installed. Package test suites were not needed because only evaluation artifacts changed.

Read-only `git status --short` (also saved as `git-status.txt`):

```text
?? dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit-data/
?? dev/research/evaluations/2026-11-07-fit-audit.md
```

No fitters, dossiers, goldens, production tests, bindings, generated package files, status/roadmap files or board entries were edited. No commits or writing Git commands were run.

## Detailed family tables

`E` is the maximum of relative reported-logLik error, demonstrated relative likelihood gain, and verified isolated-reference coefficient error. These are different diagnostics. Infinity marks an invalid/nonfinite Ok payload or an exact returned-point support exclusion, not a finite measured error. A dash means no error value was evaluated. Status 0 is native Ok; -1 is implicit Ok for a result without a status member; -997 is the audit wall guard; -998/-999 mean missing process/core observation. Other codes are native family enums. “ok” includes successful numerical checks and proved nonexistence/incompatibility stops; it is not a global certificate for arbitrary data.
